# Agents · R · part 15

978 entries, release-check to release-qa-coordinator. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **release-check**: Audits a Tabler release before the version PR is merged. Use when the user asks whether a release is ready, what blocks it, or to run the pre-release checklist. Checks the version PR, CI, the milestone, every pending changeset, the release notes intro and its images, the upgrade guide, the docs pages and the local quality gates, then reports go / no-go with concrete fixes. Read-only: it never merges, commits or pushes. ([tabler/tabler](https://github.com/tabler/tabler/blob/HEAD/.agents/agents/release-check.md) · ★42k)
